# Generated from CK3 1.21.0 landed_titles plus title_types prefixes and explicit type assignments (I36).
# Source: current native static landless titles; count: 3338
# Dynamic titles cannot be referenced before creation and are intentionally absent.

sltm_lvm_cache_one_static_uncreated_title_effect = {
    save_temporary_scope_as = sltm_lvm_static_uncreated_candidate
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_landless_type_title = yes
            is_title_created = no
            # 当前候选必须处于玩家任一持有头衔的法理下游。
            # 候选 scope 由注册表调用点保存，避免切到玩家 scope 后丢失。
            scope:sltm_lvm_cache_player = {
                any_held_title = {
                    is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = scope:sltm_lvm_static_uncreated_candidate
                }
            }
        }
        sltm_lvm_cache_title_display_type_effect = yes
        save_temporary_scope_as = sltm_lvm_static_uncreated_title
        scope:sltm_lvm_cache_player = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = sltm_lvm_all_titles
                target = scope:sltm_lvm_static_uncreated_title
            }
            change_variable = { name = sltm_lvm_title_count add = 1 }
            change_variable = { name = sltm_lvm_uncreated_title_count add = 1 }
        }
    }
}
